function useReveal() { const ref = useRef(null); const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false); useEffect(() => { const el = ref.current; if (!el) return; const io = new IntersectionObserver( (entries) => { entries.forEach((e) => { if (e.isIntersecting) { setVisible(true); io.unobserve(e.target); } }); }, { threshold: 0.15 } ); io.observe(el); return () => io.disconnect(); }, []); return [ref, visible]; } function Reveal({ children, delay = 0, className = "" }) { const [ref, visible] = useReveal(); return (
{children}

{/* MISSION */}
“Every mind deserves to learn.”
Our Mission Bridging research,
education & advocacy.

We advocate for greater investment in underfunded conditions, raise awareness through educational initiatives, and empower communities with the knowledge to recognize, understand, and advocate for their health. Rather than simply raising awareness, LEAP exists to help bridge the gap between scientific research, education, and community action through storytelling, advocacy, and meaningful engagement.

{/* FOUNDER */}
The Founder
Brittania Evelyn

Founder, LEAP

Once I learned about my family’s experience with endometriosis, I began to wonder and worry about how many other women go through this.

At the time, treatment focused on managing symptoms with birth control because there were limited options to diagnose and treat the underlying condition. Over the years, doctors conducted countless ultrasounds and tests but failed to find the answer. Unfortunately, the condition continued to worsen until it resulted in having a hysterectomy.

But my story is not a unique one. Many women live with similar conditions for years while women’s health research continues to lag behind. Despite remarkable advances in medicine, there are still diseases that lack accurate methods of diagnosis.

This realization inspired me to establish LEAP. Today, LEAP exists to help every family receive the answers, advocacy, and hope they deserve.

“Do the best you can until you know better. Then when you know better, do better.”

— Maya Angelou
{/* UNDERSTANDING ENDOMETRIOSIS */}

Endometrial-type tissue, viewed under a microscope.

Understanding Endometriosis en·do·me·tree·O·sis

Endometriosis is an often-painful condition in which tissue that is similar to the inner lining of the uterus grows outside the uterus. It remains under-researched and, for many, takes years to properly diagnose. Closing that gap is exactly why LEAP exists.

Volunteer Opportunities Three ways to put your voice to work.
Remote Advocacy
  • Email at least 3 of your local council members advocating for our cause.
  • Upload screenshot proof.
  • Send proof to: leaplearningproject@gmail.com
  • Receive a Certificate of Completion.
Social Media Advocacy
  • Create a TikTok or Instagram video promoting LEAP Learning Project.
  • Use: #leaplearningproject
  • We will review your submission.
  • Earn up to 40 verified community service hours.
Fundraising
  • Place our GoFundMe link in your bio.

To receive a Certificate of Completion for any one of these opportunities, you must submit all proof to{" "} leaplearningproject@gmail.com .
